			end

      
			assign port.clk = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk;

      
			assign port.rst = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst;

      
			assign port.rstStart = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rstStart;

      
			if (1) begin : controller

      
				reg [1:0] regPhase;

      
				reg [1:0] nextPhase;

      
				reg dcFlushReqAck;

      
				reg dcFlushComplete;

      
				reg mshrBusy;

      
				reg loadStoreBusy;

      
				always @(posedge Core.dCache.port.clk)

      
					if (Core.dCache.port.rst)

      
						regPhase <= 2'd0;

      
					else

      
						regPhase <= nextPhase;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM = 1;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM = 2;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;

      
				always @(*) begin

      
					nextPhase = regPhase;

      
					dcFlushReqAck = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					dcFlushComplete = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					mshrBusy = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					loadStoreBusy = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					case (regPhase)

      
						default: nextPhase = 2'd0;

      
						2'd0: begin

      
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_309

      
								reg signed [31:0] i;

      
								for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
									if (Core.dCache.port.mshrPhase[i] != 5'd0)

      
										mshrBusy = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							end

      
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_310

      
								reg signed [31:0] i;

      
								for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
									if (Core.dCache.port.lsuCacheGrtReg[i] || Core.dCache.port.lsuCacheGrt[i])

      
										loadStoreBusy = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							end

      
							dcFlushReqAck = !mshrBusy;

      
							if (Core.dCache.port.dcFlushReq && dcFlushReqAck)

      
								nextPhase = 2'd1;

      
						end

      
						2'd1:

      
							if (Core.dCache.port.mshrFlushComplete)

      
								nextPhase = 2'd2;

      
						2'd2: begin

      
							dcFlushComplete = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							if (Core.dCache.port.flushComplete)

      
								nextPhase = 2'd0;

      
						end

      
					endcase

      
					Core.dCache.port.dcFlushReqAck = dcFlushReqAck;

      
					Core.dCache.port.dcFlushComplete = dcFlushComplete;

      
					Core.dCache.port.dcFlushing = regPhase == 2'd1;

      
				end

      
			end

      
			if (1) begin : array

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM = 2;

      
				reg [0:1] dataArrayWE;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;

      
				reg [0:1] dataArrayByteWE [0:7];

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 9;

      
				reg [17:0] dataArrayIndex;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;

      
				reg [15:0] dataArrayIn [0:7];

      
				wire [15:0] dataArrayOut [0:7];

      
				reg [0:1] dataArrayDirtyIn;

      
				wire [0:1] dataArrayDirtyOut;

      
				reg [15:0] dataArrayByteWE_Tmp;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;

      
				reg [127:0] dataArrayInTmp;

      
				reg [127:0] dataArrayOutTmp;

      
				reg [0:1] tagArrayWE;

      
				reg [17:0] tagArrayIndex;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;

      
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 10;

      
				reg [21:0] tagArrayIn;

      
				wire [21:0] tagArrayOut;

      
				reg [8:0] rstIndex;

      
				always @(posedge Core.dCache.port.clk)

      
					if (Core.dCache.port.rstStart)

      
						rstIndex <= 1'sb0;

      
					else

      
						rstIndex <= rstIndex + 1;

      
				genvar i;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_NUM = 512;

      
				for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1

      
					BlockTrueDualPortRAM #(

      
						.ENTRY_NUM(CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_NUM),

      
						.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH)

      
					) dataArray(

      
						.clk(Core.dCache.port.clk),

      
						.we(dataArrayByteWE[i]),

      
						.rwa(dataArrayIndex),

      
						.wv(dataArrayIn[i]),

      
						.rv(dataArrayOut[i])

      
					);

      
				end

      
				BlockTrueDualPortRAM #(

      
					.ENTRY_NUM(CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_NUM),

      
					.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(1)

      
				) dirtyArray(

      
					.clk(Core.dCache.port.clk),

      
					.we(dataArrayWE),

      
					.rwa(dataArrayIndex),

      
					.wv(dataArrayDirtyIn),

      
					.rv(dataArrayDirtyOut)

      
				);

      
				BlockTrueDualPortRAM #(

      
					.ENTRY_NUM(CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_NUM),

      
					.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(11)

      
				) tagArray(

      
					.clk(Core.dCache.port.clk),

      
					.we(tagArrayWE),

      
					.rwa(tagArrayIndex),

      
					.wv(tagArrayIn),

      
					.rv(tagArrayOut)

      
				);

      
				localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
				always @(*) begin

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_311

      
						reg signed [31:0] p;

      
						for (p = 0; p < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
							begin

      
								dataArrayIndex[(1 - p) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.dCache.port.dataArrayIndexIn[p];

      
								dataArrayDirtyIn[p] = Core.dCache.port.dataArrayDirtyIn[p];

      
								dataArrayWE[p] = Core.dCache.port.dataArrayWE[p];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					dataArrayByteWE_Tmp = Core.dCache.port.dataArrayByteWE_In;

      
					dataArrayInTmp = Core.dCache.port.dataArrayDataIn;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_312

      
						reg signed [31:0] p;

      
						for (p = 0; p < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_313

      
								reg signed [31:0] i;

      
								for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
									begin

      
										dataArrayByteWE[i][p] = Core.dCache.port.dataArrayWE[p] && dataArrayByteWE_Tmp[((1 - p) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM) + i];

      
										begin : sv2v_autoblock_314

      
											reg signed [31:0] b;

      
											for (b = 0; b < 8; b = b + 1)

      
												begin

      
													dataArrayIn[i][((1 - p) * BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH) + b] = dataArrayInTmp[((1 - p) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH) + ((i * 8) + b)];

      
													dataArrayOutTmp[((1 - p) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH) + ((i * 8) + b)] = dataArrayOut[i][((1 - p) * BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH) + b];

      
												end

      
										end

      
									end

      
							end

      
					end

      
					Core.dCache.port.dataArrayDataOut = dataArrayOutTmp;

      
					Core.dCache.port.dataArrayDirtyOut = dataArrayDirtyOut;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_315

      
						reg signed [31:0] p;

      
						for (p = 0; p < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
							begin

      
								tagArrayIndex[(1 - p) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.dCache.port.tagArrayIndexIn[p];

      
								tagArrayWE[p] = Core.dCache.port.tagArrayWE[p];

      
								tagArrayIn[((1 - p) * 11) + 9-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.dCache.port.tagArrayDataIn[p];

      
								tagArrayIn[((1 - p) * 11) + 10] = Core.dCache.port.tagArrayValidIn[p];

      
								Core.dCache.port.tagArrayDataOut[(1 - p) * 10+:10] = tagArrayOut[((1 - p) * 11) + 9-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH];

      
								Core.dCache.port.tagArrayValidOut[p] = tagArrayOut[((1 - p) * 11) + 10];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					if (Core.dCache.port.rst) begin

      
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_316

      
							reg signed [31:0] p;

      
							for (p = 0; p < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
								begin

      
									begin : sv2v_autoblock_317

      
										reg signed [31:0] i;

      
										for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
											dataArrayByteWE[i][p] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
									end

      
									tagArrayWE[p] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								end

      
						end

      
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_318

      
							reg signed [31:0] i;

      
							for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
								begin

      
									dataArrayByteWE[i][0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
									dataArrayIn[i][BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH] = 8'hcd;

      
								end

      
						end

      
						dataArrayWE[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
						dataArrayIndex[CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] = rstIndex;

      
						dataArrayDirtyIn[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
						tagArrayIndex[CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] = rstIndex;

      
						tagArrayWE[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
						tagArrayIn[20-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH] = 0;

      
						tagArrayIn[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			if (1) begin : arrayArbiter

      
				localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM = 1;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM = 2;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_MUX_PORT_NUM = 4;

      
				reg req [0:3];

      
				reg grant [0:3];

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM = 2;

      
				reg [3:0] cacheArrayInSel;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;

      
				reg [3:0] cacheArrayOutSel;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
				always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_319

      
					reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;

      
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_320

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_MUX_PORT_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							begin

      
								cacheArrayOutSel[(3 - r) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
								grant[r] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							end

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_321

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							if (Core.dCache.port.dcFlushing)

      
								req[r] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							else

      
								req[r] = Core.dCache.port.lsuCacheReq[r];

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_322

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							req[r + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM] = Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheReq[r];

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_323

      
						reg signed [31:0] p;

      
						for (p = 0; p < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
								cacheArrayInSel[(1 - p) * 2+:2] = 1'sb0;

      
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_324

      
									reg signed [31:0] r;

      
									begin : sv2v_autoblock_325

      
										reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;

      
										_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;

      
										for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_MUX_PORT_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
											if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
												_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
												if (req[r]) begin

      
													req[r] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
													grant[r] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
													cacheArrayInSel[(1 - p) * 2+:2] = r;

      
													cacheArrayOutSel[(3 - r) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = p;

      
													_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
												end

      
											end

      
										if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
											_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;

      
									end

      
								end

      
							end

      
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
					end

      
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin

      
						Core.dCache.port.cacheArrayInSel = cacheArrayInSel;

      
						Core.dCache.port.cacheArrayOutSel = cacheArrayOutSel;

      
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_326

      
							reg signed [31:0] r;

      
							for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
								Core.dCache.port.lsuCacheGrt[r] = grant[r];

      
						end

      
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_327

      
							reg signed [31:0] r;

      
							for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheGrt[r] = grant[r + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM];

      
						end

      
					end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			if (1) begin : arrayMux

      
				localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM = 1;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM = 2;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_MUX_PORT_NUM = 4;

      
				reg [1:0] portIn;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM = 2;

      
				reg [1:0] portInRegTagStg [0:1];

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;

      
				reg [0:0] portOutRegTagStg [0:3];

      
				reg [0:0] portOutRegDataStg [0:3];

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 9;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;

      
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 10;

      
				reg [97:0] muxIn [0:3];

      
				reg [97:0] muxInReg [0:1];

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;

      
				reg [79:0] muxTagOut [0:3];

      
				reg [64:0] muxDataOut [0:3];

      
				reg tagHit [0:1];

      
				reg mshrConflict [0:1];

      
				reg mshrAddrHit [0:1];

      
				reg [0:0] mshrAddrHitMSHRID [0:1];

      
				reg mshrReadHit [0:1];

      
				reg [63:0] mshrReadData [0:1];

      
				reg [63:0] portMSHRData [0:1];

      
				reg portMSHRCanBeInvalid [0:1];

      
				reg [19:0] tagArrayDataOutTmp;

      
				reg [0:1] tagArrayValidOutTmp;

      
				reg [0:1] dataArrayDirtyOutTmp;

      
				reg [127:0] dataArrayDataOutTmp;

      
				always @(posedge Core.dCache.port.clk) begin

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_328

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							if (Core.dCache.port.rst) begin

      
								portInRegTagStg[i] <= 1'sb0;

      
								muxInReg[i] <= 1'sb0;

      
							end

      
							else begin

      
								portInRegTagStg[i] <= Core.dCache.port.cacheArrayInSel[(1 - i) * 2+:2];

      
								muxInReg[i] <= muxIn[Core.dCache.port.cacheArrayInSel[(1 - i) * 2+:2]];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_329

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_MUX_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							if (Core.dCache.port.rst) begin

      
								portOutRegTagStg[i] <= 1'sb0;

      
								portOutRegDataStg[i] <= 1'sb0;

      
							end

      
							else begin

      
								portOutRegTagStg[i] <= Core.dCache.port.cacheArrayOutSel[(3 - i) * 1+:1];

      
								portOutRegDataStg[i] <= portOutRegTagStg[i];

      
							end

      
					end

      
				end

      
				localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;

      
				function automatic [8:0] ToIndexPartFromFullAddr;

      
					input reg [21:0] addr;

      
					ToIndexPartFromFullAddr = addr[11:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
				endfunction

      
				function automatic [9:0] ToTagPartFromFullAddr;

      
					input reg [21:0] addr;

      
					ToTagPartFromFullAddr = addr[21:12];

      
				endfunction

      
				always @(*) begin

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_330

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							muxIn[r] = Core.dCache.port.lsuMuxIn[r];

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_331

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							muxIn[r + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM] = Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[r];

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_332

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							begin

      
								portMSHRCanBeInvalid[r] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								portMSHRData[r] = Core.dCache.port.mshrData[r];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_333

      
						reg signed [31:0] p;

      
						for (p = 0; p < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
							begin

      
								portIn = Core.dCache.port.cacheArrayInSel[(1 - p) * 2+:2];

      
								Core.dCache.port.tagArrayWE[p] = muxIn[portIn][88];

      
								Core.dCache.port.tagArrayIndexIn[p] = muxIn[portIn][97-:9];

      
								Core.dCache.port.tagArrayDataIn[p] = muxIn[portIn][87-:10];

      
								Core.dCache.port.tagArrayValidIn[p] = muxIn[portIn][77];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					tagArrayDataOutTmp = Core.dCache.port.tagArrayDataOut;

      
					tagArrayValidOutTmp = Core.dCache.port.tagArrayValidOut;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_334

      
						reg signed [31:0] p;

      
						for (p = 0; p < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
							begin

      
								mshrConflict[p] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								mshrReadHit[p] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								mshrAddrHit[p] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								mshrAddrHitMSHRID[p] = 1'sb0;

      
								mshrReadData[p] = 1'sb0;

      
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_335

      
									reg signed [31:0] m;

      
									for (m = 0; m < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; m = m + 1)

      
										if (Core.dCache.port.mshrValid[m] && (muxInReg[p][97-:9] == ToIndexPartFromFullAddr(Core.dCache.port.mshrAddr[m]))) begin

      
											mshrConflict[p] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
											if (muxInReg[p][87-:10] == ToTagPartFromFullAddr(Core.dCache.port.mshrAddr[m])) begin

      
												if (Core.dCache.port.mshrPhase[m] > (5'd17 - 1)) begin

      
													if (muxInReg[p][1])

      
														portMSHRCanBeInvalid[m] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
													mshrReadHit[p] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
												end

      
												mshrAddrHit[p] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
												mshrAddrHitMSHRID[p] = m;

      
												mshrReadData[p] = portMSHRData[m];

      
											end

      
										end

      
								end

      
								tagHit[p] = ((tagArrayDataOutTmp[(1 - p) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH] == muxInReg[p][87-:10]) && tagArrayValidOutTmp[p]) && !mshrConflict[p];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_336

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_MUX_PORT_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							begin

      
								muxTagOut[r][79-:10] = tagArrayDataOutTmp[(1 - portOutRegTagStg[r]) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH];

      
								muxTagOut[r][69] = tagArrayValidOutTmp[portOutRegTagStg[r]];

      
								muxTagOut[r][68] = tagHit[portOutRegTagStg[r]];

      
								muxTagOut[r][67] = mshrConflict[portOutRegTagStg[r]];

      
								muxTagOut[r][64] = mshrReadHit[portOutRegTagStg[r]];

      
								muxTagOut[r][66] = mshrAddrHit[portOutRegTagStg[r]];

      
								muxTagOut[r][65-:1] = mshrAddrHitMSHRID[portOutRegTagStg[r]];

      
								muxTagOut[r][63-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH] = mshrReadData[portOutRegTagStg[r]];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_337

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							Core.dCache.port.mshrCanBeInvalid[r] = portMSHRCanBeInvalid[r];

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_338

      
						reg signed [31:0] p;

      
						for (p = 0; p < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM; p = p + 1)

      
							begin

      
								Core.dCache.port.dataArrayDataIn[(1 - p) * 64+:64] = muxInReg[p][74-:64];

      
								Core.dCache.port.dataArrayDirtyIn[p] = muxInReg[p][2];

      
								Core.dCache.port.dataArrayByteWE_In[(1 - p) * 8+:8] = muxInReg[p][10-:8];

      
								Core.dCache.port.dataArrayWE[p] = muxInReg[p][75] || (muxInReg[p][76] && tagHit[p]);

      
								Core.dCache.port.dataArrayIndexIn[p] = muxInReg[p][97-:9];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					dataArrayDataOutTmp = Core.dCache.port.dataArrayDataOut;

      
					dataArrayDirtyOutTmp = Core.dCache.port.dataArrayDirtyOut;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_339

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_MUX_PORT_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							begin

      
								muxDataOut[r][64-:64] = dataArrayDataOutTmp[(1 - portOutRegDataStg[r]) * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH];

      
								muxDataOut[r][0] = dataArrayDirtyOutTmp[portOutRegDataStg[r]];

      
							end

      
					end

      
				end

      
				always @(*) begin

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_340

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							begin

      
								Core.dCache.port.lsuMuxTagOut[r] = muxTagOut[r];

      
								Core.dCache.port.lsuMuxDataOut[r] = muxDataOut[r];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_341

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							begin

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxTagOut[r] = muxTagOut[r + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM];

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxDataOut[r] = muxDataOut[r + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM];

      
							end

      
					end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			if (1) begin : memArbiter

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;

      
				reg req [0:1];

      
				reg grant [0:1];

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;

      
				reg [0:0] memInSel;

      
				reg memValid;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
				localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
				always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_342

      
					reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;

      
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_343

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							begin

      
								grant[r] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								req[r] = Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[r];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					memInSel = 1'sb0;

      
					memValid = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_344

      
						reg signed [31:0] r;

      
						for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
								if (req[r]) begin

      
									grant[r] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
									memInSel = r;

      
									memValid = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
								end

      
							end

      
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
					end

      
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin

      
						Core.dCache.port.memInSel = memInSel;

      
						Core.dCache.port.memValid = memValid;

      
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_345

      
							reg signed [31:0] r;

      
							for (r = 0; r < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; r = r + 1)

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrMemGrt[r] = grant[r];

      
						end

      
					end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			if (1) begin : memMux

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;

      
				reg [0:0] portIn;

      
				always @(*) begin

      
					portIn = Core.dCache.port.memInSel;

      
					Core.dCache.port.memAddr = Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[portIn][86-:22];

      
					Core.dCache.port.memData = Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[portIn][64-:64];

      
					Core.dCache.port.memWE = Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[portIn][0];

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_346

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][3] = Core.dCache.port.memReqAck;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][2-:2] = Core.dCache.port.memSerial;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][0-:1] = Core.dCache.port.memWSerial;

      
							end

      
					end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			if (1) begin : missHandler

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 9;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_ACCESS_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 2;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_WRITE_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 1;

      
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;

      
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;

      
				reg [265:0] nextMSHR;

      
				reg [265:0] mshr;

      
				reg portIsAllocatedByStore [0:1];

      
				wire [63:0] mergedLine [0:1];

      
				reg mshrFlushComplete [0:1];

      
				localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
				always @(posedge Core.dCache.port.clk)

      
					if (Core.dCache.port.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_347

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 132] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] <= 5'd0;

      
								mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 11] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 10] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							end

      
					end

      
					else

      
						mshr <= nextMSHR;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH = 5;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;

      
				function automatic [4:0] ToIndexSubsetPartFromFullAddr;

      
					input reg [21:0] addr;

      
					ToIndexSubsetPartFromFullAddr = addr[7:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
				endfunction

      
				always @(*) begin

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_348

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrPhase[i] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5];

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrAddrSubset[i] = ToIndexSubsetPartFromFullAddr(mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22]);

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrData[i] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64];

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrValid[i] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 132];

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrAddr[i] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					Core.dCache.port.mshrFlushComplete = mshrFlushComplete[0];

      
				end

      
				reg [63:0] portStoredLineData;

      
				reg [7:0] portStoredLineByteWE;

      
				always @(*) begin

      
					portStoredLineData = Core.dCache.port.storedLineData;

      
					portStoredLineByteWE = Core.dCache.port.storedLineByteWE;

      
				end

      
				localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 10;

      
				function automatic [21:0] BuildFullAddr;

      
					input reg [8:0] index;

      
					input reg [9:0] tag;

      
					BuildFullAddr = {tag, index, {CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH {1'b0}}};

      
				endfunction

      
				task automatic MergeStoreDataToLine;

      
					output reg [63:0] dstLine;

      
					input reg [63:0] fetchedLine;

      
					input reg [63:0] storedLine;

      
					input reg [7:0] storedDirty;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_349

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_350

      
								reg signed [31:0] b;

      
								for (b = 0; b < 8; b = b + 1)

      
									dstLine[(i * 8) + b] = (storedDirty[i] ? storedLine[(i * 8) + b] : fetchedLine[(i * 8) + b]);

      
							end

      
					end

      
				endtask

      
				function automatic [8:0] ToIndexPartFromFullAddr;

      
					input reg [21:0] addr;

      
					ToIndexPartFromFullAddr = addr[11:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
				endfunction

      
				function automatic [21:0] ToLineAddrFromFullAddr;

      
					input reg [21:0] addr;

      
					ToLineAddrFromFullAddr = {addr[21:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH], {CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH {1'b0}}};

      
				endfunction

      
				function automatic [9:0] ToTagPartFromFullAddr;

      
					input reg [21:0] addr;

      
					ToTagPartFromFullAddr = addr[21:12];

      
				endfunction

      
				always @(*) begin

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_351

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							portIsAllocatedByStore[i] = Core.dCache.port.isAllocatedByStore[i];

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_352

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								nextMSHR[(1 - i) * 133+:133] = mshr[(1 - i) * 133+:133];

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][97-:9] = ToIndexPartFromFullAddr(mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22]);

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][87-:10] = ToTagPartFromFullAddr(mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22]);

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][74-:64] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64];

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][10-:8] = {CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM {1'b1}};

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][77] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheReq[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][88] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][75] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][76] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][64-:64] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64];

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][86-:22] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 123-:22];

      
								mshrFlushComplete[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								case (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5])

      
									default:

      
										if (Core.dCache.port.initMSHR[i]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 132] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22] = Core.dCache.port.initMSHR_Addr[i];

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 101] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 126] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 124] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 78-:2] = 1'sb0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] = 1'sb0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 11] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 10] = portIsAllocatedByStore[i];

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 9] = Core.dCache.port.isUncachable[i];

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 8-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
											if (Core.dCache.port.isUncachable[i])

      
												nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd13;

      
											else

      
												nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd7;

      
										end

      
										else if (Core.dCache.port.dcFlushing && (i == 0)) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 132] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22] = 1'sb0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 101] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 126] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 124] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 78-:2] = 1'sb0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] = 1'sb0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 11] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 10] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 9] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 8-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64] = 1'sb0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd1;

      
										end

      
									5'd1: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheReq[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][97-:9] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 8-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH];

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][77] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][88] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][75] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][76] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = (Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheGrt[i] ? 5'd2 : 5'd1);

      
									end

      
									5'd2:

      
										if (Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxTagOut[i][69]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 123-:22] = BuildFullAddr(mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 8-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH], Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxTagOut[i][79-:10]);

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd3;

      
										end

      
										else begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd6;

      
										end

      
									5'd3: begin

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 124] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64] = Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxDataOut[i][64-:64];

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 126] = Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxDataOut[i][0];

      
										if (nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 126])

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd4;

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd6;

      
									end

      
									5'd4: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][86-:22] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 123-:22];

      
										if (Core.dCache.port.mshrMemGrt[i] && Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][3]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] = Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][0-:1];

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd5;

      
										end

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd4;

      
									end

      
									5'd5: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										if ((mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] && mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 126]) && !(Core.dCache.port.memAccessResponse[1] && (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] == Core.dCache.port.memAccessResponse[0-:1])))

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd5;

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd6;

      
									end

      
									5'd6: begin

      
										if (&mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 8-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 8-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
											mshrFlushComplete[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										end

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 8-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 8-:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] + 1;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 126] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 124] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 78-:2] = 1'sb0;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] = 1'sb0;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64] = 1'sb0;

      
										if (mshrFlushComplete[i]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 132] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										end

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd1;

      
									end

      
									5'd7: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheReq[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][88] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][75] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][76] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = (Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheGrt[i] ? 5'd8 : 5'd7);

      
									end

      
									5'd8:

      
										if (Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxTagOut[i][69]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 123-:22] = BuildFullAddr(ToIndexPartFromFullAddr(mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22]), Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxTagOut[i][79-:10]);

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd9;

      
										end

      
										else begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd13;

      
										end

      
									5'd9: begin

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 124] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64] = Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxDataOut[i][64-:64];

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 126] = Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxDataOut[i][0];

      
										if (nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 126])

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd10;

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd13;

      
									end

      
									5'd10: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][86-:22] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 123-:22];

      
										if (Core.dCache.port.mshrMemGrt[i] && Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][3]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] = Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][0-:1];

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd11;

      
										end

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd10;

      
									end

      
									5'd11: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										if ((mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] && mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 126]) && !(Core.dCache.port.memAccessResponse[1] && (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] == Core.dCache.port.memAccessResponse[0-:1])))

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd11;

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd13;

      
									end

      
									5'd13:

      
										if (BasicTypes_TRUE) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 125] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
											Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
											Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][86-:22] = ToLineAddrFromFullAddr(mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22]);

      
											if (Core.dCache.port.mshrMemGrt[i] && Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][3]) begin

      
												nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 78-:2] = Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][2-:2];

      
												nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd14;

      
											end

      
											else

      
												nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd13;

      
										end

      
									5'd14:

      
										if (!(Core.dCache.port.memAccessResult[66] && (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 78-:2] == Core.dCache.port.memAccessResult[65-:2])))

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd14;

      
										else begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64] = Core.dCache.port.memAccessResult[63-:64];

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 101] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
											if (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 10])

      
												nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd12;

      
											else if (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 9])

      
												nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd18;

      
											else

      
												nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd17;

      
										end

      
									5'd12: begin

      
										MergeStoreDataToLine(mergedLine[i], mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64], portStoredLineData, portStoredLineByteWE);

      
										nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 75-:64] = mergedLine[i];

      
										if (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 9])

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd15;

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd17;

      
									end

      
									5'd15: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxIn[i][86-:22] = ToLineAddrFromFullAddr(mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 100-:22]);

      
										if (Core.dCache.port.mshrMemGrt[i] && Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][3]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] = Core.dCache.port.mshrMemMuxOut[i][0-:1];

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd16;

      
										end

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd15;

      
									end

      
									5'd16: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrMemReq[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										if (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 101] && !(Core.dCache.port.memAccessResponse[1] && (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 76-:1] == Core.dCache.port.memAccessResponse[0-:1])))

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd16;

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd18;

      
									end

      
									5'd17: begin

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheReq[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][88] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][75] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][76] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheMuxIn[i][2] = mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 10];

      
										if (Core.dCache.port.mshrCacheGrt[i])

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd18;

      
										else

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd17;

      
									end

      
									5'd18:

      
										if (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 11] || mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 10]) begin

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] = 5'd0;

      
											nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 132] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
										end

      
								endcase

      
								if (Core.dCache.port.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage[i])

      
									nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 11] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
								else if (Core.dCache.port.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i])

      
									nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 11] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
								else if (Core.dCache.port.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[i])

      
									nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 11] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
								else if (Core.dCache.port.mshrCanBeInvalid[i] && (mshr[((1 - i) * 133) + 131-:5] >= 5'd17))

      
									nextMSHR[((1 - i) * 133) + 11] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							end

      
					end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;

      
			reg [63:0] storedLineData;

      
			reg [7:0] storedLineByteWE;

      
			reg [21:0] dcReadAddrRegTagStg;

      
			reg [21:0] dcReadAddrRegDataStg;

      
			reg dcReadReqReg [0:0];

      
			reg [0:1] lsuCacheGrtReg;

      
			reg dcReadUncachableReg [0:0];

      
			reg dcWriteReqReg;

      
			reg [21:0] dcWriteAddrReg;

      
			reg dcWriteUncachableReg;

      
			reg lsuLoadHasAllocatedMSHR [0:0];

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;

      
			reg [0:0] lsuLoadMSHRID [0:0];

      
			reg lsuStoreHasAllocatedMSHR [0:0];

      
			reg [0:0] lsuStoreMSHRID [0:0];

      
			reg lsuMSHRAddrHit [0:0];

      
			reg [0:0] lsuMSHRAddrHitMSHRID [0:0];

      
			reg lsuMSHRReadHit [0:0];

      
			reg [63:0] lsuMSHRReadData [0:0];

      
			reg lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage [0:1];

      
			reg lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalid [0:0];

      
			reg lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage [0:1];

      
			reg lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue [0:1];

      
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
			always @(posedge port.clk)

      
				if (port.rst) begin

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_353

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							dcReadReqReg[i] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_354

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							lsuCacheGrtReg[i] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					end

      
					dcWriteReqReg <= 1'sb0;

      
				end

      
				else begin

      
					lsuCacheGrtReg <= port.lsuCacheGrt;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_355

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								dcReadReqReg[i] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadReq[i];

      
								dcReadAddrRegTagStg[i * 22+:22] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadAddr[i];

      
								dcReadUncachableReg[i] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadUncachable[i];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					dcReadAddrRegDataStg <= dcReadAddrRegTagStg;

      
					dcWriteReqReg <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteReq;

      
					dcWriteAddrReg <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteAddr;

      
					dcWriteUncachableReg <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteUncachable;

      
				end

      
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH = 32;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN = 0;

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN = CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM;

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 9;

      
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 10;

      
			function automatic [8:0] ToIndexPartFromFullAddr;

      
				input reg [21:0] addr;

      
				ToIndexPartFromFullAddr = addr[11:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
			endfunction

      
			function automatic [9:0] ToTagPartFromFullAddr;

      
				input reg [21:0] addr;

      
				ToTagPartFromFullAddr = addr[21:12];

      
			endfunction

      
			always @(*) begin

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_356

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i];

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_357

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							port.lsuCacheReq[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadReq[i];

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][88] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][97-:9] = ToIndexPartFromFullAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadAddr[i]);

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][87-:10] = ToTagPartFromFullAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadAddr[i]);

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][77] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][74-:64] = 1'sb0;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][10-:8] = 1'sb0;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][75] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][76] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][1] = lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i];

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_358

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							hit[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							if (port.lsuMuxTagOut[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN][68] && lsuCacheGrtReg[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_BEGIN])

      
								hit[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_359

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadData[i] = port.lsuMuxDataOut[i][64-:64];

      
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadHit[i] = hit[i];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_360

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							storedLineData = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteData;

      
							storedLineByteWE = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteByteWE;

      
							port.lsuCacheReq[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteReq;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][88] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][97-:9] = ToIndexPartFromFullAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteAddr);

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][87-:10] = ToTagPartFromFullAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteAddr);

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][77] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][74-:64] = storedLineData;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][10-:8] = storedLineByteWE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][75] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][76] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							port.lsuMuxIn[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteReqAck = port.lsuCacheGrt[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_361

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							hit[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							if (port.lsuMuxTagOut[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN][68] && lsuCacheGrtReg[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN])

      
								hit[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteHit = hit[i + CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN];

      
						end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			always @(*) begin

      
				port.storedLineData = storedLineData;

      
				port.storedLineByteWE = storedLineByteWE;

      
			end

      
			always @(*) begin

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_362

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							missReq[i] = (((!hit[i] && !port.lsuMuxTagOut[i][67]) && dcReadReqReg[i]) && lsuCacheGrtReg[i]) && !Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadCancelFromMT_Stage[i];

      
							missAddr[i] = dcReadAddrRegTagStg[i * 22+:22];

      
							missIsUncachable[i] = dcReadUncachableReg[i];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_363

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN; i < 2; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							missReq[i] = ((!hit[i] && !port.lsuMuxTagOut[i][67]) && dcWriteReqReg) && lsuCacheGrtReg[i];

      
							missAddr[i] = dcWriteAddrReg;

      
							missIsUncachable[i] = dcWriteUncachableReg;

      
						end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			reg mshrConflict [0:1];

      
			reg portInitMSHR [0:1];

      
			reg [21:0] portInitMSHR_Addr [0:1];

      
			reg portIsAllocatedByStore [0:1];

      
			reg portIsUncachable [0:1];

      
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_364

      
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;

      
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_365

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							lsuLoadHasAllocatedMSHR[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							lsuLoadMSHRID[i] = 1'sb0;

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_366

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							lsuStoreHasAllocatedMSHR[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							lsuStoreMSHRID[i] = 1'sb0;

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_367

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							lsuMSHRAddrHit[i] = port.lsuMuxTagOut[i][66];

      
							lsuMSHRAddrHitMSHRID[i] = port.lsuMuxTagOut[i][65-:1];

      
							lsuMSHRReadHit[i] = port.lsuMuxTagOut[i][64];

      
							lsuMSHRReadData[i] = port.lsuMuxTagOut[i][63-:64];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_368

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						mshrConflict[i] = port.lsuMuxTagOut[i][67];

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_369

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_370

      
							reg signed [31:0] m;

      
							for (m = 0; m < i; m = m + 1)

      
								if (missReq[m] && (ToIndexPartFromFullAddr(missAddr[i]) == ToIndexPartFromFullAddr(missAddr[m])))

      
									mshrConflict[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_371

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							portInitMSHR[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							portInitMSHR_Addr[i] = 1'sb0;

      
							portIsAllocatedByStore[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							portIsUncachable[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_372

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
							if (!missReq[i] || mshrConflict[i])

      
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b01;

      
							if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin : sv2v_autoblock_373

      
								reg signed [31:0] m;

      
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_374

      
									reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;

      
									_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;

      
									for (m = 0; m < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; m = m + 1)

      
										if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
											if (!port.mshrValid[m] && !portInitMSHR[m]) begin

      
												portInitMSHR[m] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
												portInitMSHR_Addr[m] = missAddr[i];

      
												portIsUncachable[m] = missIsUncachable[i];

      
												if (i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM) begin

      
													lsuLoadHasAllocatedMSHR[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
													lsuLoadMSHRID[i] = m;

      
												end

      
												else begin

      
													lsuStoreHasAllocatedMSHR[i - CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
													lsuStoreMSHRID[i - CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM] = m;

      
													portIsAllocatedByStore[m] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
												end

      
												_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
											end

      
										end

      
									if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
										_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;

      
								end

      
							end

      
						end

      
					if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
				end

      
				if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_375

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								port.initMSHR[i] = portInitMSHR[i];

      
								port.initMSHR_Addr[i] = portInitMSHR_Addr[i];

      
								port.isAllocatedByStore[i] = portIsAllocatedByStore[i];

      
								port.isUncachable[i] = portIsUncachable[i];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					port.lsuCacheGrtReg = lsuCacheGrtReg;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_376

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadBusy[i] = mshrConflict[i];

      
					end

      
					Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteBusy = mshrConflict[CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_BEGIN];

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_377

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrAddrHit[i] = lsuMSHRAddrHit[i];

      
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrAddrHitMSHRID[i] = lsuMSHRAddrHitMSHRID[i];

      
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadHit[i] = lsuMSHRReadHit[i];

      
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadData[i] = lsuMSHRReadData[i];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_378

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.loadHasAllocatedMSHR[i] = lsuLoadHasAllocatedMSHR[i];

      
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.loadMSHRID[i] = lsuLoadMSHRID[i];

      
							end

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_379

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
							begin

      
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeHasAllocatedMSHR[i] = lsuStoreHasAllocatedMSHR[i];

      
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeMSHRID[i] = lsuStoreMSHRID[i];

      
							end

      
					end

      
				end

      
			end

      
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_380

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					begin

      
						lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage[i];

      
						lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i];

      
						lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[i];

      
					end

      
			end

      
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_381

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					begin

      
						port.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage[i] = lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage[i];

      
						port.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i] = lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i];

      
						port.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[i] = lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[i];

      
					end

      
			end

      
			always @(*) begin

      
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[87] = port.memValid;

      
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[86] = port.memWE;

      
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[85-:22] = port.memAddr;

      
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[63-:64] = port.memData;

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_382

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrPhase[i] = port.mshrPhase[i];

      
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrAddrSubset[i] = port.mshrAddrSubset[i];

      
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrValid[i] = port.mshrValid[i];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				port.memReqAck = Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReqAck[3];

      
				port.memSerial = Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReqAck[2-:2];

      
				port.memAccessResult = Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessResult;

      
				port.memWSerial = Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReqAck[0-:1];

      
				port.memAccessResponse = Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessResponse;

      
			end

      
			always @(*) begin

      
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcFlushReqAck = port.dcFlushReqAck;

      
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcFlushComplete = port.dcFlushComplete;

      
				port.dcFlushReq = Core.cacheSystemIF.dcFlushReq;

      
				port.flushComplete = Core.cacheSystemIF.flushComplete;

      
			end

      
		end

      
		if (1) begin : memRwStage

      
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;

      
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;

      
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;

      
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;

      
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;

      
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;

      
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;

      
			reg [247:0] pipeReg;

      
			always @(posedge Core.ctrlIF.clk)

      
				if (Core.ctrlIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_383

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 123] <= 1'sb0;

      
				end

      
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])

      
					pipeReg <= Core.maStageIF.nextStage;

      
			reg stall;

      
			reg clear;

      
			reg flush [0:1];

      
			reg update [0:1];

      
			reg valid [0:1];

      
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;

      
			reg [70:0] alWriteData [0:1];

      
			reg [3:0] execState [0:1];

      
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;

      
				input reg detectRange;

      
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;

      
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;

      
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;

      
				if (!detectRange)

      
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin

      
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))

      
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
					else

      
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
				end

      
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin

      
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))

      
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))

      
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
					else

      
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
				end

      
				else

      
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			endfunction

      
			always @(*) begin

      
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];

      
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_384

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							valid[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 123];

      
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 122-:6]);

      
							update[i] = ((!stall && !clear) && valid[i]) && !flush[i];

      
							alWriteData[i][70-:6] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 122-:6];

      
							alWriteData[i][64-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 116-:4];

      
							alWriteData[i][60-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 112-:4];

      
							alWriteData[i][1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							alWriteData[i][0] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 33];

      
							if (update[i])

      
								alWriteData[i][56-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 37-:4];

      
							else

      
								alWriteData[i][56-:4] = 4'b0000;

      
							execState[i] = alWriteData[i][56-:4];

      
							alWriteData[i][52-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 108-:32];

      
							alWriteData[i][33-:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 76-:32];

      
							Core.activeListIF.memWrite[i] = update[i];

      
							Core.activeListIF.memWriteData[i] = alWriteData[i];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_385

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							Core.registerFileIF.memDstRegWE[i] = update[i] && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 44];

      
							Core.registerFileIF.memDstRegNum[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 43-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
							Core.registerFileIF.memDstRegData[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 124) + 32-:33];

      
						end

      
				end

      
			end

      
		end

      
		if (1) begin : registerFile

      
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH = 5;

      
			localparam REG_READ_NUM = 10;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
			localparam REG_WRITE_NUM = 4;

      
			reg [0:3] regWE;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;

      
			reg [23:0] dstRegNum;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;

      
			reg [131:0] dstRegData;

      
			reg [59:0] srcRegNum;

      
			wire [329:0] srcRegData;

      
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(

      
				.ENTRY_NUM(BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM),

      
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(33),

      
				.READ_NUM(REG_READ_NUM),

      
				.WRITE_NUM(REG_WRITE_NUM)

      
			) phyReg(

      
				.clk(Core.registerFileIF.clk),

      
				.we(regWE),

      
				.wa(dstRegNum),

      
				.wv(dstRegData),

      
				.ra(srcRegNum),

      
				.rv(srcRegData)

      
			);

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;

      
			reg [5:0] regRstIndex;

      
			always @(posedge Core.registerFileIF.clk)

      
				if (Core.registerFileIF.rstStart)

      
					regRstIndex <= 0;

      
				else

      
					regRstIndex <= regRstIndex + REG_WRITE_NUM;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
			always @(*) begin

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_386

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							regWE[i] = Core.registerFileIF.intDstRegWE[i];

      
							dstRegNum[(3 - i) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.intDstRegNum[i];

      
							dstRegData[(3 - i) * 33+:33] = Core.registerFileIF.intDstRegData[i];

      
							srcRegNum[(9 - (i * 2)) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegNumA[i];

      
							srcRegNum[(9 - ((i * 2) + 1)) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegNumB[i];

      
							Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegDataA[i] = srcRegData[(9 - (i * 2)) * 33+:33];

      
							Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegDataB[i] = srcRegData[(9 - ((i * 2) + 1)) * 33+:33];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_387

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							regWE[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.complexDstRegWE[i];

      
							dstRegNum[(3 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.complexDstRegNum[i];

      
							dstRegData[(3 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 33+:33] = Core.registerFileIF.complexDstRegData[i];

      
							srcRegNum[(9 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 2)) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.complexSrcRegNumA[i];

      
							srcRegNum[(9 - (((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 2) + 1)) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.complexSrcRegNumB[i];

      
							Core.registerFileIF.complexSrcRegDataA[i] = srcRegData[(9 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 2)) * 33+:33];

      
							Core.registerFileIF.complexSrcRegDataB[i] = srcRegData[(9 - (((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 2) + 1)) * 33+:33];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_388

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							srcRegNum[(9 - (((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 2)) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegNumA[i];

      
							srcRegNum[(9 - ((((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 2) + 1)) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegNumB[i];

      
							Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegDataA[i] = srcRegData[(9 - (((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 2)) * 33+:33];

      
							Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegDataB[i] = srcRegData[(9 - ((((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 2) + 1)) * 33+:33];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_389

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							regWE[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.memDstRegWE[i];

      
							dstRegNum[(3 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.registerFileIF.memDstRegNum[i];

      
							dstRegData[(3 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 33+:33] = Core.registerFileIF.memDstRegData[i];

      
						end

      
				end

      
				if (Core.registerFileIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_390

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < REG_WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
						begin

      
							regWE[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							dstRegNum[(3 - i) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = regRstIndex + i;

      
							dstRegData[((3 - i) * 33) + 31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = 'h0;

      
							dstRegData[((3 - i) * 33) + 32] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
						end

      
				end

      
			end

      
		end

      
		if (1) begin : bypassController

      
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;

      
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;

      
			function automatic [5:0] SelectReg;

      
				input reg [5:0] regNum;

      
				input reg read;

      
				input reg [13:0] intEX;

      
				input reg [13:0] intWB;

      
				input reg [6:0] memMA;

      
				input reg [6:0] memWB;

      
				reg [5:0] ret;

      
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;

      
				begin

      
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
					ret[5] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					ret[4-:2] = 2'd0;

      
					ret[2-:1] = 0;

      
					ret[0-:BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE] = 0;

      
					ret[1-:1] = 0;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_391

      
						reg signed [31:0] i;

      
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
								if ((read && intEX[(1 - i) * 7]) && (regNum == intEX[((1 - i) * 7) + 6-:6])) begin

      
									ret[5] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
									ret[4-:2] = 2'd0;

      
									ret[2-:1] = i;

      
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
								end

      
								if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)

      
									if ((read && intWB[(1 - i) * 7]) && (regNum == intWB[((1 - i) * 7) + 6-:6])) begin

      
										ret[5] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										ret[4-:2] = 2'd1;

      
										ret[2-:1] = i;

      
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
									end

      
							end

      
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
					end

      
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin

      
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_392

      
							reg signed [31:0] i;

      
							for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
								if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
									if ((read && memMA[i * 7]) && (regNum == memMA[(i * 7) + 6-:6])) begin

      
										ret[5] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
										ret[4-:2] = 2'd2;

      
										ret[0-:BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE] = i;

      
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
									end

      
									if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)

      
										if ((read && memWB[i * 7]) && (regNum == memWB[(i * 7) + 6-:6])) begin

      
											ret[5] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
											ret[4-:2] = 2'd3;

      
											ret[0-:BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE] = i;

      
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
										end

      
								end

      
							if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
						end

      
						if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin

      
							SelectReg = ret;

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b11;

      
						end

      
					end

      
				end

      
			endfunction

      
			wire clk;

      
			wire rst;

      
			assign clk = Core.bypassNetworkIF.clk;

      
			assign rst = Core.bypassNetworkIF.rst;

      
			reg [6:0] intRR [0:1];
