			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeBrFunct3(condCode, brFunct3);

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = condCode;

      
			opInfo[32-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH] = OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement(isfR);

      
			opInfo[47-:15] = 1'sb0;

      
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeBranch;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] brOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitBranch(brOp, isf);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(brOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	localparam BasicTypes_LSCALAR_NUM = 32;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_LSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;

      
	localparam MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER = 5'h00;

      
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeComplexOpFunct3;

      
		output reg [1:0] mulCode;

      
		output reg [1:0] divCode;

      
		input reg [2:0] funct3;

      
		case (funct3)

      
			3'b000: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b00;

      
				divCode = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
			3'b001: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b01;

      
				divCode = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
			3'b010: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b10;

      
				divCode = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
			3'b011: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b11;

      
				divCode = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
			3'b100: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b00;

      
				divCode = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
			3'b101: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b00;

      
				divCode = 2'b01;

      
			end

      
			3'b110: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b00;

      
				divCode = 2'b10;

      
			end

      
			3'b111: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b00;

      
				divCode = 2'b11;

      
			end

      
			default: begin

      
				mulCode = 2'b00;

      
				divCode = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
		endcase

      
	endtask

      
	function automatic [6:0] sv2v_cast_7;

      
		input reg [6:0] inp;

      
		sv2v_cast_7 = inp;

      
	endfunction

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitComplexOp;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumA;

      
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumB;

      
		input reg [4:0] dstRegNum;

      
		input reg unsupported;

      
		reg [31:0] isfR;

      
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;

      
		reg [6:0] opFunct7;

      
		reg [2:0] rv32mFunct3;

      
		reg [6:0] rv32mFunct7;

      
		reg isMul;

      
		reg [1:0] mulCode;

      
		reg [1:0] divCode;

      
		begin

      
			isfR = isf;

      
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);

      
			opFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);

      
			rv32mFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);

      
			rv32mFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);

      
			isMul = rv32mFunct3 < 3'b100;

      
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dstRegNum;

      
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumA;

      
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumB;

      
			opInfo[47] = rv32mFunct3 > 3'b000;

      
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeComplexOpFunct3(mulCode, divCode, rv32mFunct3);

      
			opInfo[46-:2] = mulCode;

      
			opInfo[44-:2] = divCode;

      
			opInfo[42-:16] = 1'sb0;

      
			opInfo[26-:14] = 1'sb0;

      
			opInfo[8] = (dstRegNum != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = (isMul ? 3'b000 : 3'b001);

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[6] = unsupported;

      
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeComplexOp;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] complexOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitComplexOp(complexOp, isf, isf[19-:5], isf[24-:5], isf[11-:5], BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(complexOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			mid = mid + 1;

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitIllegalOp;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg illegalPC;

      
		reg [31:0] isfSystem;

      
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;

      
		reg [11:0] opFunct12;

      
		reg [49:0] systemOp;

      
		reg undefined;

      
		begin

      
			systemOp[49-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			systemOp[44-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			systemOp[39-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			systemOp[31] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			systemOp[30-:19] = 1'sb0;

      
			systemOp[11-:12] = 1'sb0;

      
			undefined = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			systemOp[34-:3] = (illegalPC ? 3'b100 : 3'b011);

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b110;

      
			opInfo[62-:50] = systemOp;

      
			opInfo[8] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[7] = undefined;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeIllegal;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		input illegalPC;

      
		reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitIllegalOp(opInfo, illegalPC);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_3

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(opInfo, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	localparam MicroOpTypes_LINK_REGISTER = 5'h01;

      
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target;

      
		input reg [31:0] isfJAL;

      
		OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target = {isfJAL[31], isfJAL[19-:8], isfJAL[20], isfJAL[30-:10]};

      
	endfunction

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitJAL;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [31:0] isfU;

      
		begin

      
			isfU = isf;

      
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfU[11-:5];

      
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			opInfo[8] = (isfU[11-:5] != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b10;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[32-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH] = OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target(isf);

      
			opInfo[47-:15] = 0;

      
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeJAL;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] brOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitJAL(brOp, isf);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_4

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(brOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = (brOp[8] && (brOp[62-:5] == 5'h01) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetJALR_Target;

      
		input reg [31:0] isfJALR;

      
		OpFormatTypes_GetJALR_Target = {{8 {isfJALR[31]}}, isfJALR[31-:12]};

      
	endfunction

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitJALR;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [31:0] isfI;

      
		begin

      
			isfI = isf;

      
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfI[11-:5];

      
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfI[19-:5];

      
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 0;

      
			opInfo[8] = (isfI[11-:5] != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b011;

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[32-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH] = OpFormatTypes_GetJALR_Target(isfI);

      
			opInfo[47-:15] = 1'sb0;

      
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeJALR;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] brOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitJALR(brOp, isf);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_5

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(brOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = (brOp[8] && (brOp[62-:5] == 5'h01) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			insnInfo[2] = ((brOp[57-:5] == 5'h01) && (brOp[62-:5] == 5'h00) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeMemAccessMode;

      
		output reg [2:0] memAccessMode;

      
		input reg [2:0] funct3;

      
		case (funct3)

      
			3'b000: begin

      
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
			3'b001: begin

      
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			end

      
			3'b010: begin

      
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b10;

      
			end

      
			3'b100: begin

      
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
			3'b101: begin

      
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			end

      
			default: begin

      
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
		endcase

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitMemOp;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [31:0] isfS;

      
		reg [31:0] isfI;

      
		reg [2:0] memFunct3;

      
		reg isLoad;

      
		reg [2:0] memAccessMode;

      
		begin

      
			isfS = isf;

      
			isfI = isf;

      
			memFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfS[14-:3]);

      
			isLoad = isfI[6-:7] == 7'b0000011;

      
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = (isLoad ? isfI[11-:5] : {5 {1'sb0}});

      
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfI[19-:5];

      
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = (isLoad ? {5 {1'sb0}} : isfS[24-:5]);

      
			opInfo[42-:8] = 1'sb0;

      
			opInfo[34-:10] = 1'sb0;

      
			opInfo[8] = (isfI[11-:5] != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER) & isLoad;

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = (isLoad ? 2'b01 : 2'b00);

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = (isLoad ? 3'b000 : 3'b001);

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[24-:12] = (isLoad ? isfI[31-:12] : {isfS[31-:7], isfS[11-:5]});

      
			opInfo[47] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			opInfo[46] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeMemAccessMode(memAccessMode, memFunct3);

      
			opInfo[45-:3] = memAccessMode;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeMemOp;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] memOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitMemOp(memOp, isf);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_6

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(memOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitMiscMemOp;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [31:0] isfMiscMem;

      
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;

      
		reg [49:0] miscMemOp;

      
		begin

      
			isfMiscMem = isf;

      
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfMiscMem[14-:3]);

      
			miscMemOp[49-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			miscMemOp[44-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			miscMemOp[39-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			miscMemOp[32-:15] = 1'sb0;

      
			miscMemOp[17-:18] = 1'sb0;

      
			if (opFunct3 == 3'b000) begin

      
				miscMemOp[34] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
				miscMemOp[33] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			end

      
			else if (opFunct3 == 3'b001) begin

      
				miscMemOp[34] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
				miscMemOp[33] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			end

      
			else begin

      
				miscMemOp[34] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
				miscMemOp[33] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			end

      
			opInfo[62-:50] = miscMemOp;

      
			opInfo[8] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b101;

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			if (miscMemOp[34])

      
				opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			else

      
				opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeMiscMem;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] miscMemOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitMiscMemOp(miscMemOp, isf);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_7

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(miscMemOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeOpFunct3;

      
		output reg [3:0] aluCode;

      
		input reg [2:0] funct3;

      
		input reg [6:0] funct7;

      
		case (funct3)

      
			3'b000:

      
				if (funct7 == 7'b0000000)

      
					aluCode = 4'b0000;

      
				else

      
					aluCode = 4'b0001;

      
			3'b010: aluCode = 4'b0010;

      
			3'b011: aluCode = 4'b0011;

      
			3'b100: aluCode = 4'b0100;

      
			3'b110: aluCode = 4'b0110;

      
			3'b111: aluCode = 4'b0111;

      
			3'b001: aluCode = 4'b0111;

      
			3'b101: aluCode = 4'b0111;

      
			default: aluCode = 4'b0111;

      
		endcase

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitOp;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumA;

      
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumB;

      
		input reg [4:0] dstRegNum;

      
		input reg unsupported;

      
		reg [31:0] isfR;

      
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;

      
		reg [6:0] opFunct7;

      
		reg [6:0] shiftFunct7;

      
		reg [29:0] intOperandImmShift;

      
		reg isShift;

      
		reg [3:0] aluCode;

      
		begin

      
			isfR = isf;

      
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);

      
			opFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);

      
			shiftFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);

      
			isShift = (opFunct3 == 3'b001) || (opFunct3 == 3'b101);

      
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dstRegNum;

      
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumA;

      
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumB;

      
			opInfo[43] = 1'b1;

      
			intOperandImmShift[29-:5] = (isShift ? isfR[24-:5] : {5 {1'sb0}});

      
			intOperandImmShift[24-:2] = (opFunct3 == 3'b001 ? 2'b00 : ((opFunct3 == 3'b101) && (shiftFunct7 == 7'b0000000) ? 2'b01 : ((opFunct3 == 3'b101) && (shiftFunct7 == 7'b0100000) ? 2'b10 : 2'b11)));

      
			intOperandImmShift[22] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			intOperandImmShift[21-:20] = 1'sb0;

      
			intOperandImmShift[1-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[42-:BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH] = intOperandImmShift;

      
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeOpFunct3(aluCode, opFunct3, opFunct7);

      
			opInfo[47-:4] = aluCode;

      
			opInfo[8] = (dstRegNum != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = (isShift ? 3'b001 : 3'b000);

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[6] = unsupported;

      
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeOp;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] intOp;

      
		reg [70:0] shiftOp;

      
		reg [70:0] rijOp;

      
		reg [70:0] selectOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitOp(intOp, isf, isf[19-:5], isf[24-:5], isf[11-:5], BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_8

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(intOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			mid = mid + 1;

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_I_TypeImmExtention;

      
		input reg [31:0] isfI;

      
		OpFormatTypes_I_TypeImmExtention = {{8 {isfI[31]}}, isfI[31-:12]};

      
	endfunction

      
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeOpImmFunct3;

      
		output reg [3:0] aluCode;

      
		input reg [2:0] funct3;

      
		case (funct3)

      
			3'b000: aluCode = 4'b0000;

      
			3'b010: aluCode = 4'b0010;

      
			3'b011: aluCode = 4'b0011;

      
			3'b100: aluCode = 4'b0100;

      
			3'b110: aluCode = 4'b0110;

      
			3'b111: aluCode = 4'b0111;

      
			3'b001: aluCode = 4'b0111;

      
			3'b101: aluCode = 4'b0111;

      
			default: aluCode = 4'b0111;

      
		endcase

      
	endtask

      
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_ShamtExtention;

      
		input reg [31:0] isfR;

      
		OpFormatTypes_ShamtExtention = {15'h0000, isfR[24-:5]};

      
	endfunction

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitOpImm;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumA;

      
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumB;

      
		input reg [4:0] dstRegNum;

      
		input reg unsupported;

      
		reg [31:0] isfI;

      
		reg [31:0] isfR;

      
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;

      
		reg [6:0] shiftFunct7;

      
		reg [29:0] intOperandImmShift;

      
		reg isShift;

      
		reg [3:0] aluCode;

      
		begin

      
			isfI = isf;

      
			isfR = isf;

      
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);

      
			shiftFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);

      
			isShift = (opFunct3 == 3'b001) || (opFunct3 == 3'b101);

      
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dstRegNum;

      
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumA;

      
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumB;

      
			opInfo[43] = 1'b0;

      
			intOperandImmShift[29-:5] = (isShift ? isfR[24-:5] : {5 {1'sb0}});

      
			intOperandImmShift[24-:2] = (opFunct3 == 3'b001 ? 2'b00 : ((opFunct3 == 3'b101) && (shiftFunct7 == 7'b0000000) ? 2'b01 : ((opFunct3 == 3'b101) && (shiftFunct7 == 7'b0100000) ? 2'b10 : 2'b11)));

      
			intOperandImmShift[22] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			intOperandImmShift[21-:20] = (isShift ? OpFormatTypes_ShamtExtention(isfR) : OpFormatTypes_I_TypeImmExtention(isfI));

      
			intOperandImmShift[1-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[42-:BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH] = intOperandImmShift;

      
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeOpImmFunct3(aluCode, opFunct3);

      
			opInfo[47-:4] = aluCode;

      
			opInfo[8] = (dstRegNum != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = (isShift ? 3'b001 : 3'b000);

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[6] = unsupported;

      
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeOpImm;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] intOp;

      
		reg [70:0] shiftOp;

      
		reg [70:0] rijOp;

      
		reg [70:0] selectOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitOpImm(intOp, isf, isf[19-:5], 0, isf[11-:5], BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_9

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(intOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			mid = mid + 1;

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitCSR_Op;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [31:0] isfSystem;

      
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;

      
		reg [49:0] memOp;

      
		begin

      
			isfSystem = isf;

      
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfSystem[14-:3]);

      
			memOp[49-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfSystem[11-:5];

      
			memOp[44-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfSystem[19-:5];

      
			memOp[39-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			memOp[21-:10] = 1'sb0;

      
			memOp[34] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			memOp[33] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			memOp[32-:3] = 1'sb0;

      
			case (opFunct3)

      
				default: memOp[23-:2] = 2'b01;

      
				3'b001, 3'b101: memOp[23-:2] = 2'b01;

      
				3'b010, 3'b110: memOp[23-:2] = 2'b10;

      
				3'b011, 3'b111: memOp[23-:2] = 2'b11;

      
			endcase

      
			memOp[11-:12] = isfSystem[31-:12];

      
			memOp[24] = (|{opFunct3 == 3'b001, opFunct3 == 3'b010, opFunct3 == 3'b011} ? BasicTypes_FALSE : BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			memOp[29-:5] = isfSystem[19-:5];

      
			opInfo[62-:50] = memOp;

      
			opInfo[8] = (isfSystem[11-:5] != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b100;

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[7] = (opFunct3 == 3'b100 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	function automatic [11:0] sv2v_cast_12;

      
		input reg [11:0] inp;

      
		sv2v_cast_12 = inp;

      
	endfunction

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitSystemOp;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [31:0] isfSystem;

      
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;

      
		reg [11:0] opFunct12;

      
		reg [49:0] systemOp;

      
		reg undefined;

      
		begin

      
			isfSystem = isf;

      
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfSystem[14-:3]);

      
			opFunct12 = sv2v_cast_12(isfSystem[31-:12]);

      
			systemOp[49-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			systemOp[44-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			systemOp[39-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;

      
			systemOp[31] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			systemOp[30-:19] = 1'sb0;

      
			systemOp[11-:12] = 1'sb0;

      
			undefined = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			if (opFunct12 == 12'b000100000101) begin

      
				opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;

      
				opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b000;

      
				systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b001;

      
			end

      
			else begin

      
				case (sv2v_cast_12(isfSystem[31-:12]))

      
					12'b000000000000: systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b000;

      
					12'b000000000001: systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b001;

      
					12'b001100000010: systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b010;

      
					default: begin

      
						systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b001;

      
						undefined = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
					end

      
				endcase

      
				opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;

      
				opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b110;

      
			end

      
			opInfo[62-:50] = systemOp;

      
			opInfo[8] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[7] = undefined;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeSystem;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			if (sv2v_cast_3(isf[14-:3]) == 3'b000)

      
				RISCV_EmitSystemOp(opInfo, isf);

      
			else

      
				RISCV_EmitCSR_Op(opInfo, isf);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_10

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(opInfo, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_EmitUTypeInst;

      
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumA;

      
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumB;

      
		input reg [4:0] dstRegNum;

      
		input reg unsupported;

      
		reg [31:0] isfU;

      
		reg [29:0] intOperandImmShift;

      
		begin

      
			isfU = isf;

      
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dstRegNum;

      
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumA;

      
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumB;

      
			opInfo[43] = 1'b1;

      
			intOperandImmShift[29-:5] = 1'sb0;

      
			intOperandImmShift[24-:2] = 2'b11;

      
			intOperandImmShift[22] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			intOperandImmShift[21-:20] = isfU[31-:20];

      
			intOperandImmShift[1-:2] = 2'b11;

      
			opInfo[42-:BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH] = intOperandImmShift;

      
			opInfo[47-:4] = 4'b0000;

      
			opInfo[8] = (dstRegNum != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			opInfo[12-:2] = (isf[6-:7] == 7'b0010111 ? 2'b10 : 2'b00);

      
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;

      
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;

      
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b000;

      
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;

      
			opInfo[6] = unsupported;

      
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeUTypeInst;

      
		output reg [212:0] microOps;

      
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;

      
		input reg [31:0] isf;

      
		reg [70:0] intOp;

      
		reg [1:0] mid;

      
		begin

      
			RISCV_EmitUTypeInst(intOp, isf, 0, 0, isf[11-:5], BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
			mid = 0;

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_11

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);

      
			end

      
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(intOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
			mid = mid + 1;

      
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	endtask

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		isf = insn;

      
		rv32mFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isf[31-:7]);

      
		insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		case (isf[6-:7])

      
			7'b0000011, 7'b0100011:

      
				RISCV_DecodeMemOp(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			7'b1100011:

      
				RISCV_DecodeBranch(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			7'b1100111:

      
				RISCV_DecodeJALR(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			7'b1101111:

      
				RISCV_DecodeJAL(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			7'b0010011:

      
				RISCV_DecodeOpImm(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			7'b0110011:

      
				if (rv32mFunct7 == 7'b0000001)

      
					RISCV_DecodeComplexOp(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
				else

      
					RISCV_DecodeOp(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			7'b0010111, 7'b0110111:

      
				RISCV_DecodeUTypeInst(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			7'b0001111:

      
				RISCV_DecodeMiscMem(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			7'b1110011:

      
				RISCV_DecodeSystem(microOps, insnInfo, insn);

      
			default:

      
				RISCV_DecodeIllegal(microOps, insnInfo, insn, illegalPC);

      
		endcase

      
		undefined = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_12

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				if (microOps[(i * 71) + 7] || microOps[(i * 71) + 6])

      
					undefined = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
		if (undefined || illegalPC)

      
			RISCV_DecodeIllegal(microOps, insnInfo, insn, illegalPC);

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module DecodedBranchResolver (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	stall,

      
	decodeComplete,

      
	insnValidIn,

      
	isf,

      
	brPredIn,

      
	pc,

      
	insnInfo,

      
	insnValidOut,

      
	insnFlushed,

      
	flush,

      
	brPredOut,

      
	recoveredPC

      
);

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire stall;

      
	input wire decodeComplete;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;

      
	input wire [0:1] insnValidIn;

      
	input wire [63:0] isf;

      
	localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;

      
	localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;

      
	localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;

      
	input wire [63:0] brPredIn;

      
	input wire [37:0] pc;

      
	input wire [9:0] insnInfo;

      
	output reg [0:1] insnValidOut;

      
	output reg [0:1] insnFlushed;

      
	output reg flush;

      
	output reg [63:0] brPredOut;

      
	output reg [18:0] recoveredPC;

      
	parameter RAS_ENTRY_NUM = 4;

      
	reg [18:0] ras [0:RAS_ENTRY_NUM - 1];

      
	reg [18:0] nextRAS;

      
	reg pushRAS;

      
	reg popRAS;

      
	reg [$clog2(RAS_ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] rasPtr;

      
	reg [$clog2(RAS_ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] nextRAS_Ptr;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < RAS_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					ras[i] <= 1'sb0;

      
			end

      
			rasPtr <= 1'sb0;

      
		end

      
		else if (!stall && decodeComplete) begin

      
			if (pushRAS)

      
				ras[nextRAS_Ptr] <= nextRAS;

      
			rasPtr <= nextRAS_Ptr;

      
		end

      
	reg [18:0] decodedPC [0:1];

      
	reg [18:0] nextPC [0:1];

      
	reg [31:0] isfU [0:1];

      
	reg addrCheck;

      
	reg addrIncorrect;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE;

      
	reg [0:0] addrCheckLane;

      
	reg [1:0] brTargetType [0:1];

      
	reg addrMismatch [0:1];

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;

      
	localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;

      
	function automatic [31:0] OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement;

      
		input reg [19:0] brDisp;

      
		OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement = {{11 {brDisp[19]}}, brDisp, 1'b0};

      
	endfunction

      
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement;

      
		input reg [31:0] isfBr;

      
		OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement = {{9 {isfBr[31]}}, isfBr[7], isfBr[30-:6], isfBr[11-:4]};

      
	endfunction

      
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target;

      
		input reg [31:0] isfJAL;

      
		OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target = {isfJAL[31], isfJAL[19-:8], isfJAL[20], isfJAL[30-:10]};

      
	endfunction

      
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;

      
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
		flush = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		recoveredPC = 1'sb0;

      
		pushRAS = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		popRAS = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		nextRAS = 0;

      
		nextRAS_Ptr = rasPtr;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_3

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin

      
					insnValidOut[i] = insnValidIn[i];

      
					insnFlushed[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					brPredOut[(1 - i) * 32+:32] = brPredIn[i * 32+:32];

      
					isfU[i] = isf[i * 32+:32];

      
				end

      
		end

      
		addrCheck = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		addrIncorrect = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		addrCheckLane = 1'sb0;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_4

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				if (insnInfo[(i * 5) + 1])

      
					brTargetType[i] = 2'd1;

      
				else if (insnInfo[(i * 5) + 4])

      
					brTargetType[i] = 2'd2;

      
				else if (insnInfo[i * 5])

      
					brTargetType[i] = 2'd3;

      
				else

      
					brTargetType[i] = 2'd0;

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_5

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
					if (!insnValidIn[i])

      
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)

      
						if (!insnInfo[(i * 5) + 4] && brPredIn[(i * 32) + 12]) begin

      
							addrCheckLane = i;

      
							addrCheck = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							addrIncorrect = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
						end

      
						else if ((brTargetType[i] == 2'd1) && ((isfU[i][6-:7] == 7'b1101111) || brPredIn[(i * 32) + 12])) begin

      
							pushRAS = insnInfo[(i * 5) + 3];

      
							addrCheckLane = i;

      
							addrCheck = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
						end

      
						else if (brTargetType[i] == 2'd2) begin

      
							pushRAS = insnInfo[(i * 5) + 3];

      
							popRAS = insnInfo[(i * 5) + 2];

      
							if (popRAS) begin

      
								addrCheckLane = i;

      
								addrCheck = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
							end

      
							else if (pushRAS) begin

      
								addrCheckLane = i;

      
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
							end

      
						end

      
						else if (brTargetType[i] == 2'd3) begin

      
							addrCheckLane = i;

      
							addrCheck = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							addrIncorrect = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
						end

      
				end

      
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
		end

      
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_6

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
					begin

      
						nextPC[i] = pc[(1 - i) * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] + BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH;

      
						if (brTargetType[i] == 2'd1) begin

      
							if (isfU[i][6-:7] == 7'b1101111)

      
								decodedPC[i] = pc[(1 - i) * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] + OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement(OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target(isfU[i]));

      
							else

      
								decodedPC[i] = pc[(1 - i) * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] + OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement(OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement(isfU[i]));

      
						end

      
						else if (brTargetType[i] == 2'd2)

      
							decodedPC[i] = ras[rasPtr];

      
						else

      
							decodedPC[i] = nextPC[i];

      
					end

      
			end

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_7

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
					addrMismatch[i] = brPredIn[(i * 32) + 31-:19] != decodedPC[i];

      
			end

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_8

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
					if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
						if (((addrMismatch[i] && addrCheck) || addrIncorrect) && (addrCheckLane == i)) begin

      
							flush = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							brPredOut[((1 - i) * 32) + 31-:19] = decodedPC[i];

      
							brPredOut[((1 - i) * 32) + 12] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
						end

      
					end

      
				if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
			end

      
			if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin

      
				recoveredPC = decodedPC[addrCheckLane];

      
				nextRAS = nextPC[addrCheckLane];

      
				if (pushRAS)

      
					nextRAS_Ptr = rasPtr + 1;

      
				else if (popRAS)

      
					nextRAS_Ptr = rasPtr - 1;

      
				else

      
					nextRAS_Ptr = rasPtr;

      
				if (flush) begin : sv2v_autoblock_9

      
					reg signed [31:0] i;

      
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
						if (i > addrCheckLane) begin

      
							insnValidOut[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							insnFlushed[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
						end

      
				end

      
			end

      
		end

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module ReadyBitTable (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	rstStart,

      
	stall,

      
	wakeup,

      
	wakeupDstValid,

      
	wakeupDstRegNum,

      
	dispatch,

      
	dispatchedDstValid,

      
	dispatchedDstRegNum,

      
	dispatchedSrcValid,

      
	dispatchedSrcRegNum,

      
	dispatchedSrcReady

      
);

      
	parameter SRC_OP_NUM = 2;

      
	parameter REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;

      
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 32;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire rstStart;

      
	input wire stall;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
	localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;

      
	input wire [0:3] wakeup;

      
	input wire [0:3] wakeupDstValid;

      
	input wire [(4 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] wakeupDstRegNum;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;

      
	input wire [0:1] dispatch;

      
	input wire [0:1] dispatchedDstValid;

      
	input wire [(2 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] dispatchedDstRegNum;

      
	input wire [(2 * SRC_OP_NUM) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcValid;

      
	input wire [((2 * SRC_OP_NUM) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcRegNum;

      
	output reg [(2 * SRC_OP_NUM) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcReady;

      
	localparam READY_WRITE_NUM = 6;

      
	localparam READY_READ_NUM = BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH * SRC_OP_NUM;

      
	reg [0:5] readyWE;

      
	reg [0:5] readyWV;

      
	reg [(6 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] readyWA;

      
	wire [0:READY_READ_NUM - 1] readyRV;

      
	reg [(READY_READ_NUM * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] readyRA;

      
	DistributedMultiPortRAM #(

      
		.ENTRY_NUM(1 << REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH),

      
		.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(1),

      
		.READ_NUM(READY_READ_NUM),

      
		.WRITE_NUM(READY_WRITE_NUM)

      
	) radyBitTable(

      
		.clk(clk),

      
		.we(readyWE),

      
		.wa(readyWA),

      
		.wv(readyWV),

      
		.ra(readyRA),

      
		.rv(readyRV)

      
	);

      
	reg [REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] resetIndex;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rstStart)

      
			resetIndex <= 0;

      
		else

      
			resetIndex <= resetIndex + 1;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin

      
					readyWA[(5 - i) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = wakeupDstRegNum[(3 - i) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
					readyWV[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
					readyWE[i] = !stall && (wakeup[i] && wakeupDstValid[i]);

      
				end

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin

      
					readyWA[(5 - (i + SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dispatchedDstRegNum[(1 - i) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
					readyWV[i + SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
					readyWE[i + SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH] = dispatch[i] && dispatchedDstValid[i];

      
				end

      
		end

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_3

      
				reg signed [31:0] i;

      
				for (i = 0; i < READY_WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
					readyWE[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
			end

      
			readyWA[5 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = resetIndex;

      
			readyWV[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			readyWE[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_4

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_5

      
					reg signed [31:0] j;

      
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
						begin

      
							readyRA[((READY_READ_NUM - 1) - ((i * SRC_OP_NUM) + j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
							if (dispatchedSrcValid[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] == BasicTypes_FALSE)

      
								dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
							else begin

      
								dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] = readyRV[(i * SRC_OP_NUM) + j];

      
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_6

      
									reg signed [31:0] k;

      
									for (k = 0; k < SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH; k = k + 1)

      
										if ((wakeup[k] && wakeupDstValid[k]) && (wakeupDstRegNum[(3 - k) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]))

      
											dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
								end

      
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_7

      
									reg signed [31:0] k;

      
									for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)

      
										if ((dispatch[k] && dispatchedDstValid[k]) && (dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == dispatchedDstRegNum[(1 - k) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]))

      
											dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
								end

      
							end

      
						end

      
				end

      
		end

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module SourceCAM (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	stall,

      
	dispatch,

      
	dispatchPtr,

      
	dispatchedSrcRegNum,

      
	dispatchedSrcReady,

      
	wakeup,

      
	wakeupDstValid,

      
	wakeupDstRegNum,

      
	opReady

      
);

      
	parameter SRC_OP_NUM = 1;

      
	parameter REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire stall;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;

      
	input wire [0:1] dispatch;

      
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;

      
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;

      
	input wire [7:0] dispatchPtr;

      
	input wire [((2 * SRC_OP_NUM) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcRegNum;

      
	input wire [(2 * SRC_OP_NUM) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcReady;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
	localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;

      
	input wire [0:3] wakeup;

      
	input wire [0:3] wakeupDstValid;

      
	input wire [(4 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] wakeupDstRegNum;

      
	output reg [15:0] opReady;

      
	reg srcReady [0:15][0:SRC_OP_NUM - 1];

      
	reg nextSrcReady [0:15][0:SRC_OP_NUM - 1];

      
	reg [REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] srcRegNum [0:15][0:SRC_OP_NUM - 1];

      
	reg match [0:15][0:SRC_OP_NUM - 1];

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	always @(posedge clk) begin

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
					reg signed [31:0] j;

      
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
						srcReady[i][j] <= (rst ? BasicTypes_FALSE : nextSrcReady[i][j]);

      
				end

      
		end

      
		if (rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_3

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_4

      
					reg signed [31:0] j;

      
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
						srcRegNum[i][j] <= 0;

      
				end

      
		end

      
		else begin : sv2v_autoblock_5

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				if (dispatch[i]) begin : sv2v_autoblock_6

      
					reg signed [31:0] j;

      
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
						srcRegNum[dispatchPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][j] <= dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];

      
				end

      
		end

      
	end

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_7

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_8

      
					reg signed [31:0] j;

      
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
						begin

      
							match[i][j] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_9

      
								reg signed [31:0] k;

      
								for (k = 0; k < SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH; k = k + 1)

      
									if (wakeup[k] && wakeupDstValid[k])

      
										match[i][j] = match[i][j] || (wakeupDstRegNum[(3 - k) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == srcRegNum[i][j]);

      
							end

      
						end

      
				end

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_10

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				begin

      
					opReady[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_11

      
						reg signed [31:0] j;

      
						for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
							opReady[i] = opReady[i] && (match[i][j] || srcReady[i][j]);

      
					end

      
				end

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_12

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_13

      
					reg signed [31:0] j;

      
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
						if (stall)

      
							nextSrcReady[i][j] = srcReady[i][j];

      
						else

      
							nextSrcReady[i][j] = srcReady[i][j] || match[i][j];

      
				end

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_14

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_15

      
					reg signed [31:0] j;

      
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
						if (dispatch[i])

      
							nextSrcReady[dispatchPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][j] = dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)];

      
				end

      
		end

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module ProducerMatrix (

      
	clk,

      
	stall,

      
	dispatch,

      
	dispatchedSrcRegReady,

      
	dispatchedSrcRegPtr,

      
	dependStoreBitVector,

      
	dispatchPtr,

      
	wakeupDstVector,

      
	opReady

      
);

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire stall;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;

      
	input wire [0:1] dispatch;

      
	localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_SOURCE_REG_NUM = 2;

      
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_SRC_REG_NUM = MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_SOURCE_REG_NUM;

      
	input wire [3:0] dispatchedSrcRegReady;

      
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;

      
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;

      
	input wire [15:0] dispatchedSrcRegPtr;

      
	input [31:0] dependStoreBitVector;

      
	input wire [7:0] dispatchPtr;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;

      
	localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;

      
	input wire [79:0] wakeupDstVector;

      
	output reg [0:15] opReady;

      
	reg [255:0] nextMatrix;

      
	reg [255:0] matrix;

      
	always @(posedge clk) matrix <= nextMatrix;

      
	reg [31:0] dispatchVector;

      
	reg [15:0] wakeupVector;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		nextMatrix = matrix;

      
		wakeupVector = 1'sb0;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
			reg signed [31:0] w;

      
			for (w = 0; w < 5; w = w + 1)

      
				wakeupVector = wakeupVector | wakeupDstVector[(4 - w) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM];

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_3

      
					reg signed [31:0] j;

      
					for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
						nextMatrix[(i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) + j] = (wakeupVector[j] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : nextMatrix[(i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) + j]);

      
				end

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_4

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				opReady[i] = !(|nextMatrix[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM]);

      
		end

      
		if (stall)

      
			nextMatrix = matrix;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_5

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin

      
					dispatchVector[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = 1'sb0;

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_6

      
						reg signed [31:0] j;

      
						for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_SRC_REG_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
							if (!dispatchedSrcRegReady[((1 - i) * 2) + (1 - j)])

      
								dispatchVector[(i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) + dispatchedSrcRegPtr[((i * 2) + j) * 4+:4]] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
					end

      
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_7

      
						reg signed [31:0] j;

      
						for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; j = j + 1)

      
							if (dependStoreBitVector[((1 - i) * 16) + j])

      
								dispatchVector[(i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) + j] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
					end

      
				end

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_8

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				if (dispatch[i])

      
					nextMatrix[dispatchPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = dispatchVector[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM];

      
		end

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module ICacheArray (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	rstStart,

      
	we,

      
	writeIndex,

      
	writeTag,

      
	writeLineData,

      
	readIndex,

      
	readTag,

      
	readLineData,

      
	hit,

      
	valid

      
);

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire rstStart;

      
	input wire we;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM = 2;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 8;

      
	input wire [7:0] writeIndex;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;

      
	localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 11;

      
	input wire [10:0] writeTag;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;

      
	input wire [63:0] writeLineData;

      
	input wire [7:0] readIndex;

      
	input wire [10:0] readTag;

      
	output reg [63:0] readLineData;

      
	output reg hit;

      
	output reg valid;

      
	reg weWay;

      
	reg [7:0] rstIndex;

      
	reg [7:0] writeWayIndex;

      
	wire [75:0] readWayData;

      
	reg [75:0] writeWayData;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_NUM = 256;

      
	BlockDualPortRAM #(

      
		.ENTRY_NUM(CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_NUM),

      
		.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(76)

      
	) tagValidArray(

      
		.clk(clk),

      
		.we(weWay),

      
		.wa(writeWayIndex),

      
		.wv(writeWayData),

      
		.ra(readIndex),

      
		.rv(readWayData)

      
	);

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			weWay = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			writeWayIndex = rstIndex;

      
			writeWayData[11] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			weWay = we;

      
			writeWayIndex = writeIndex;

      
			writeWayData[11] = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
		writeWayData[10-:CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH] = writeTag;

      
		writeWayData[75-:64] = writeLineData;

      
		readLineData = readWayData[75-:64];

      
		valid = readWayData[11];

      
		hit = valid && (readWayData[10-:CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH] == readTag);

      
	end

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rstStart)

      
			rstIndex <= 1'sb0;

      
		else

      
			rstIndex <= rstIndex + 1;

      
endmodule

      
module ICacheNRUStateArray (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	rstStart,

      
	writeIndex,

      
	writeWay,

      
	writeHit,

      
	writeNRUState,

      
	readIndex,

      
	readNRUState,

      
	rstIndex

      
);

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire rstStart;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM = 2;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 8;

      
	input wire [7:0] writeIndex;

      
	input wire [0:0] writeWay;

      
	input wire writeHit;

      
	input wire [1:0] writeNRUState;

      
	input wire [7:0] readIndex;

      
	output wire [1:0] readNRUState;

      
	output reg [7:0] rstIndex;

      
	reg we;

      
	reg [7:0] writeNRUStateIndex;

      
	reg [1:0] writeNRUStateData;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_NUM = 256;

      
	BlockDualPortRAM #(

      
		.ENTRY_NUM(CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_NUM),

      
		.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM)

      
	) nruStateArray(

      
		.clk(clk),

      
		.we(we),

      
		.wa(writeNRUStateIndex),

      
		.wv(writeNRUStateData),

      
		.ra(readIndex),

      
		.rv(readNRUState)

      
	);

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*)

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			we = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
			writeNRUStateIndex = rstIndex;

      
			writeNRUStateData = 1'sb0;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			we = writeHit;

      
			writeNRUStateIndex = writeIndex;

      
			writeNRUStateData = writeNRUState;

      
		end

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rstStart)

      
			rstIndex <= 1'sb0;

      
		else

      
			rstIndex <= rstIndex + 1;

      
endmodule

      
module ICacheHitLogic (

      
	hitIn,

      
	headWordPtr,

      
	hitOut

      
);

      
	input wire hitIn;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;

      
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_INSN_NUM = 2;

      
	input wire [0:0] headWordPtr;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;

      
	output reg [0:1] hitOut;

      
	reg [1:0] wordPtr [0:1];

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
		reg signed [31:0] i;

      
		for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
			begin

      
				wordPtr[i] = headWordPtr + i;

      
				if (wordPtr[i][1])

      
					hitOut[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
				else

      
					hitOut[i] = hitIn;

      
			end

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module VictimWaySelecter (

      
	valid,

      
	victimCandidateWayPtr,

      
	victimWayPtr

      
);

      
	parameter WAY_NUM = 2;

      
	input wire [WAY_NUM - 1:0] valid;

      
	input wire [$clog2(WAY_NUM) - 1:0] victimCandidateWayPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(WAY_NUM) - 1:0] victimWayPtr;

      
	reg existInvalidWay;

      
	reg [$clog2(WAY_NUM) - 1:0] invalidWayPtr;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;

      
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
		existInvalidWay = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		invalidWayPtr = 0;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < WAY_NUM; i = i + 1)

      
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin

      
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
					if (!valid[i]) begin

      
						existInvalidWay = BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
						invalidWayPtr = i;

      
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;

      
					end

      
				end

      
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)

      
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;

      
		end

      
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)

      
			victimWayPtr = (existInvalidWay ? invalidWayPtr : victimCandidateWayPtr);

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module ResetController (

      
	clk,

      
	rstTrigger,

      
	locked,

      
	rst,

      
	rstStart

      
);

      
	parameter CYCLE_OF_RESET_SEQUENCE = 10000;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rstTrigger;

      
	input wire locked;

      
	output reg rst;

      
	output reg rstStart;

      
	reg [$clog2(CYCLE_OF_RESET_SEQUENCE):0] count;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(posedge clk or posedge rstTrigger)

      
		if (rstTrigger || !locked) begin

      
			count <= 0;

      
			rst <= BasicTypes_TRUE;

      
		end

      
		else if (rst) begin

      
			count <= count + 1;

      
			rst <= (count >= CYCLE_OF_RESET_SEQUENCE ? BasicTypes_FALSE : BasicTypes_TRUE);

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			count <= 0;

      
			rst <= BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
		end

      
	always @(*) rstStart = (rst && (count == 0) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
endmodule

      
module FlipFlop (

      
	out,

      
	in,

      
	clk,

      
	rst

      
);

      
	parameter FF_WIDTH = 32;

      
	parameter RESET_VALUE = 0;

      
	output wire [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] out;

      
	input wire [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] in;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	reg [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] body;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst == 1)

      
			body <= RESET_VALUE;

      
		else

      
			body <= in;

      
	assign out = body;

      
endmodule

      
module FlipFlopWE (

      
	out,

      
	in,

      
	we,

      
	clk,

      
	rst

      
);

      
	parameter FF_WIDTH = 32;

      
	parameter RESET_VALUE = 0;

      
	output wire [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] out;

      
	input wire [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] in;

      
	input wire we;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	reg [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] body;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst == 1)

      
			body <= RESET_VALUE;

      
		else if (we)

      
			body <= in;

      
		else

      
			body <= body;

      
	assign out = body;

      
endmodule

      
module FreeList (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	push,

      
	pop,

      
	pushedData,

      
	full,

      
	empty,

      
	headData

      
);

      
	parameter SIZE = 1;

      
	parameter ENTRY_WIDTH = 1;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire push;

      
	input wire pop;

      
	input wire [ENTRY_WIDTH - 1:0] pushedData;

      
	output wire full;

      
	output wire empty;

      
	output reg [ENTRY_WIDTH - 1:0] headData;

      
	localparam BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);

      
	wire [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] headPtr;

      
	wire [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] tailPtr;

      
	QueuePointer #(

      
		.SIZE(SIZE),

      
		.INITIAL_HEAD_PTR(0),

      
		.INITIAL_TAIL_PTR(0),

      
		.INITIAL_COUNT(SIZE)

      
	) pointer(

      
		.clk(clk),

      
		.rst(rst),

      
		.push(push),

      
		.pop(pop),

      
		.full(full),

      
		.empty(empty),

      
		.headPtr(headPtr),

      
		.tailPtr(tailPtr)

      
	);

      
	reg [ENTRY_WIDTH - 1:0] freeList [SIZE - 1:0];

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < SIZE; i = i + 1)

      
				freeList[i] <= i;

      
		end

      
		else if (push)

      
			freeList[tailPtr] <= pushedData;

      
	always @(*) headData = freeList[headPtr];

      
endmodule

      
module MultiWidthFreeList (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	rstStart,

      
	push,

      
	pop,

      
	pushedData,

      
	count,

      
	poppedData

      
);

      
	parameter SIZE = 16;

      
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 4;

      
	parameter PUSH_WIDTH = 4;

      
	parameter POP_WIDTH = 4;

      
	parameter INITIAL_LENGTH = SIZE;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire rstStart;

      
	input wire [0:PUSH_WIDTH - 1] push;

      
	input wire [0:POP_WIDTH - 1] pop;

      
	input wire [(PUSH_WIDTH * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] pushedData;

      
	output wire [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;

      
	output reg [(POP_WIDTH * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] poppedData;

      
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(SIZE);

      
	wire [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] headPtr;

      
	wire [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] tailPtr;

      
	reg [$clog2(PUSH_WIDTH):0] pushCount;

      
	reg [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popCount;

      
	MultiWidthQueuePointer #(

      
		.SIZE(SIZE),

      
		.INITIAL_HEAD_PTR(0),

      
		.INITIAL_TAIL_PTR(INITIAL_LENGTH),

      
		.INITIAL_COUNT(INITIAL_LENGTH),

      
		.PUSH_WIDTH(PUSH_WIDTH),

      
		.POP_WIDTH(POP_WIDTH)

      
	) queuePointer(

      
		.clk(clk),

      
		.rst(rst),

      
		.push(pushCount != 0),

      
		.pop(popCount != 0),

      
		.pushCount(pushCount),

      
		.popCount(popCount),

      
		.headPtr(headPtr),

      
		.tailPtr(tailPtr),

      
		.count(count)

      
	);

      
	reg [0:PUSH_WIDTH - 1] we;

      
	reg [(PUSH_WIDTH * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;

      
	reg [(PUSH_WIDTH * INDEX_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wa;

      
	wire [(POP_WIDTH * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;

      
	reg [(POP_WIDTH * INDEX_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] ra;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rstIndex;

      
	DistributedMultiBankRAM #(

      
		.ENTRY_NUM(SIZE),

      
		.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),

      
		.READ_NUM(POP_WIDTH),

      
		.WRITE_NUM(PUSH_WIDTH)

      
	) freeList(

      
		.clk(clk),

      
		.wa(wa),

      
		.we(we),

      
		.wv(wv),

      
		.ra(ra),

      
		.rv(rv)

      
	);

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rstStart)

      
			rstIndex <= 0;

      
		else if (rstIndex >= (SIZE - PUSH_WIDTH))

      
			rstIndex <= 0;

      
		else

      
			rstIndex <= rstIndex + PUSH_WIDTH;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		pushCount = 0;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < PUSH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin

      
					if ((tailPtr + pushCount) >= SIZE)

      
						wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = (tailPtr + pushCount) - SIZE;

      
					else

      
						wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = tailPtr + pushCount;

      
					wv[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = pushedData[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];

      
					we[i] = push[i];

      
					if (push[i])

      
						pushCount = pushCount + 1;

      
				end

      
		end

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < POP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				if ((headPtr + i) >= SIZE)

      
					ra[((POP_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = (headPtr + i) - SIZE;

      
				else

      
					ra[((POP_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = headPtr + i;

      
		end

      
		popCount = 0;

      
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_3

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < POP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin

      
					poppedData[((POP_WIDTH - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rv[((POP_WIDTH - 1) - popCount) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];

      
					if (pop[i])

      
						popCount = popCount + 1;

      
				end

      
		end

      
		if (rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_4

      
			reg signed [31:0] i;

      
			for (i = 0; i < PUSH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)

      
				begin

      
					wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = rstIndex + i;

      
					wv[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rstIndex + i;

      
					we[i] = (wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] >= rstIndex) && (wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] < SIZE);

      
				end

      
		end

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module QueuePointer (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	push,

      
	pop,

      
	full,

      
	empty,

      
	headPtr,

      
	tailPtr

      
);

      
	parameter SIZE = 1;

      
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;

      
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;

      
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire push;

      
	input wire pop;

      
	output reg full;

      
	output reg empty;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;

      
	localparam BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHeadStorage;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHeadStorage;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTailStorage;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTailStorage;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			regHeadStorage <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;

      
			regTailStorage <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;

      
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			regHeadStorage <= nextHeadStorage;

      
			regTailStorage <= nextTailStorage;

      
			regCount <= nextCount;

      
		end

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		full = (regCount == SIZE ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
		empty = (regCount == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
		nextHeadStorage = regHeadStorage;

      
		nextTailStorage = regTailStorage;

      
		nextCount = regCount;

      
		if (push) begin

      
			if (regTailStorage == (SIZE - 1))

      
				nextTailStorage = 0;

      
			else

      
				nextTailStorage = nextTailStorage + 1;

      
			nextCount = nextCount + 1;

      
		end

      
		if (pop) begin

      
			if (regHeadStorage == (SIZE - 1))

      
				nextHeadStorage = 0;

      
			else

      
				nextHeadStorage = nextHeadStorage + 1;

      
			nextCount = nextCount - 1;

      
		end

      
		headPtr = regHeadStorage;

      
		tailPtr = regTailStorage;

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module QueuePointerWithEntryCount (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	push,

      
	pop,

      
	full,

      
	empty,

      
	headPtr,

      
	tailPtr,

      
	count

      
);

      
	parameter SIZE = 1;

      
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;

      
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;

      
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire push;

      
	input wire pop;

      
	output reg full;

      
	output reg empty;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;

      
	localparam BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHeadStorage;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHeadStorage;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTailStorage;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTailStorage;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;

      
	reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			regHeadStorage <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;

      
			regTailStorage <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;

      
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			regHeadStorage <= nextHeadStorage;

      
			regTailStorage <= nextTailStorage;

      
			regCount <= nextCount;

      
		end

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		full = (regCount == SIZE ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
		empty = (regCount == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);

      
		nextHeadStorage = regHeadStorage;

      
		nextTailStorage = regTailStorage;

      
		nextCount = regCount;

      
		if (push) begin

      
			if (regTailStorage == (SIZE - 1))

      
				nextTailStorage = 0;

      
			else

      
				nextTailStorage = nextTailStorage + 1;

      
			nextCount = nextCount + 1;

      
		end

      
		if (pop) begin

      
			if (regHeadStorage == (SIZE - 1))

      
				nextHeadStorage = 0;

      
			else

      
				nextHeadStorage = nextHeadStorage + 1;

      
			nextCount = nextCount - 1;

      
		end

      
		headPtr = regHeadStorage;

      
		tailPtr = regTailStorage;

      
		count = regCount;

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module MultiWidthQueuePointer (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	push,

      
	pop,

      
	pushCount,

      
	popCount,

      
	headPtr,

      
	tailPtr,

      
	count

      
);

      
	parameter SIZE = 1;

      
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;

      
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;

      
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;

      
	parameter PUSH_WIDTH = 1;

      
	parameter POP_WIDTH = 1;

      
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;

      
	parameter ENABLE_SPECIFIED_ENTRY_NUM = BasicTypes_FALSE;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire push;

      
	input wire pop;

      
	input wire [$clog2(PUSH_WIDTH):0] pushCount;

      
	input wire [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popCount;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;

      
	localparam INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHead;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHead;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTail;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTail;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			regHead <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;

      
			regTail <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;

      
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			regHead <= nextHead;

      
			regTail <= nextTail;

      
			regCount <= nextCount;

      
		end

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		nextTail = regTail;

      
		if (push) begin

      
			nextTail = nextTail + pushCount;

      
			if (nextTail >= SIZE)

      
				nextTail = nextTail - SIZE;

      
		end

      
	end

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		nextHead = regHead;

      
		if (pop) begin

      
			nextHead = nextHead + popCount;

      
			if (nextHead >= SIZE)

      
				nextHead = nextHead - SIZE;

      
		end

      
	end

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		nextCount = regCount;

      
		if (push)

      
			nextCount = nextCount + pushCount;

      
		if (pop)

      
			nextCount = nextCount - popCount;

      
		headPtr = regHead;

      
		tailPtr = regTail;

      
		count = regCount;

      
	end

      
endmodule

      
module SetTailMultiWidthQueuePointer (

      
	clk,

      
	rst,

      
	push,

      
	pop,

      
	pushCount,

      
	popCount,

      
	setTail,

      
	setTailPtr,

      
	headPtr,

      
	tailPtr,

      
	count

      
);

      
	parameter SIZE = 1;

      
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;

      
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;

      
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;

      
	parameter PUSH_WIDTH = 1;

      
	parameter POP_WIDTH = 1;

      
	input wire clk;

      
	input wire rst;

      
	input wire push;

      
	input wire pop;

      
	input wire [$clog2(PUSH_WIDTH):0] pushCount;

      
	input wire [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popCount;

      
	input wire setTail;

      
	input wire [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] setTailPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;

      
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;

      
	localparam INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHead;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHead;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTail;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTail;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] roundedSetTailPtr;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;

      
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;

      
	always @(posedge clk)

      
		if (rst) begin

      
			regHead <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;

      
			regTail <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;

      
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;

      
		end

      
		else begin

      
			regHead <= nextHead;

      
			regTail <= nextTail;

      
			regCount <= nextCount;

      
		end

      
	always @(*) begin

      
		nextTail = regTail;

      
		if (push) begin
